### bioindicator: abundanceratio(400, 1000) = 40 — BIOINDICATOR — LIVING THINGS AS A MEASURE OF THEIR ENVIRONMENT, AS ARITHMETIC (chosen by the ecological-survey registry, not by hand). ### bundle: chunks(100, 30) = 4 — BUNDLE — A JS BUILD BUNDLE, AS ARITHMETIC.
